campaign\\\",\\n# D&D REST service\\n\\n## Run and verify\\n\\nThe server targets Rust 1.97.0 and uses the Rust standard library only. Its\\ndurable store is accessed through the locally installed `sqlite3` executable.\\nStart it in the foreground with a port selected through `PORT`:\\n\\n```sh\\nPORT=8080 ./run.sh\\n```\\n\\nIt listens only on `127.0.0.1`. In another terminal, check readiness with:\\n\\n```sh\\ncurl -i http://127.0.0.1:8080/health\\n```\\n\\nThe successful response body is `{\\\"ok\\\":true}`. Run the regression suite without\\nstarting the server using `cargo test`. `run.sh` deliberately blocks after it\\nexecutes the compiled server, so it is not a build-only check.\\n\\n## Files and request flow\\n\\n- `run.sh` compiles `src/main.rs` with Rust 2024 edition and runs the server in\\n  the foreground.\\n- `Cargo.toml` declares the dependency-free crate and test target.\\n- `src/main.rs` contains the complete application, organized into data types,\\n  the `Storage` persistence adapter, state encoding, HTTP startup/routing,\\n  domain handlers, JSON parsing, response writing, and focused unit tests.\\n\\n`main` binds the listener, opens `game.db`, restores the in-memory users and\\ncombat sessions, then processes one TCP connection at a time. `read_request`\\nis the HTTP framing boundary: it accepts a bounded header/body and returns an\\nowned `HttpRequest`. `handle` is the routing boundary. It checks parameterized\\nresource routes before exact method/path routes, while the focused helper\\nfunctions below it implement the individual domain operations. `respond` writes\\nevery JSON HTTP response.\\n\\nThe custom `Json` parser and its `*_field` helpers form the validation boundary.\\nResponse JSON is assembled in fixed field order; preserve that ordering because\\nit is observable and keeps output deterministic without `serde`.\\n\\n## State and persistence\\n\\n`Storage` is the only persistence adapter. It creates the schema and invokes\\nthe platform `sqlite3` executable for relational reads and writes, so the Rust\\ncrate itself remains dependency-free. `game.db` contains:\\n\\n- compendium tables for monsters, tags, and items;\\n- campaign tables for characters, events, quests/milestones, factions, NPCs,\\n  inventory/equipment, crafting projects, and scheduled sessions/attendance;\\n- play-campaign tables for memberships, runtime turns, documents, events, and\\n  turn nudges;\\n- `game_state`, a versioned BLOB for registered users and combat sessions; and\\n- `schema_meta`, currently schema version 1.\\n\\nThe `DNDSTATE1`/`DNDSTATE2` encoder sorts map keys before saving. Preserve its\\nformat and validation: it is read on the next server start. It contains only\\nregistered users and standalone combat sessions; successful auth and combat\\nmutations save it immediately. Relational-domain methods write directly through\\n`Storage`. `/v1/storage/reset` recreates the tables and clears the corresponding\\nin-memory state.\\n\\n## API/domain groupings\\n\\n- Core rules: health, storage status/reset, dice, ability checks, encounter XP,\\n  and initiative.\\n- Characters and PHB rules: ability modifiers, proficiency, derived stats,\\n  spell slots, long rests, and equipment load.\\n- Authentication and combat: registration/login plus combat sessions, turns,\\n  and conditions.\\n- Compendium: monster and item creation with slug lookups.\\n- Campaign management: campaigns, characters, events, quests, factions, NPCs,\\n  inventory, equipment, crafting, schedules, analytics, audits, exports, and\\n  relationship/state summaries.\\n- Play campaigns: DM-owned lobbies, player membership, campaign documents,\\n  narrated turns, player actions, GM resolutions, nudges, and player/GM turn\\n  context.\\n- DM tools: encounter building, deterministic tier-1 loot parcels, and session\\n  recaps derived from persisted campaign and compendium data.\\n\\n## Safe extension and testing conventions\\n\\nKeep the service stdlib-only: use `TcpListener`/`TcpStream`, the existing JSON\\nhelpers, and no Cargo dependencies or HTTP crates. Treat exact paths, response\\nfield order, error bodies/statuses, validation limits, and persistence formats\\nas public behavior.\\n\\nAdd a route in `handle`, keep its domain logic in a focused helper, and add the\\ncorresponding persistence operation to `Storage` instead of embedding SQL in a\\nhandler. Unit tests live in the `#[cfg(test)]` module at the end of\\n`src/main.rs`; add focused assertions there, run `cargo test`, and exercise\\npersistence-sensitive routes with `curl` against a fresh database. Do not start\\n`./run.sh` for a build-only self.sql(&format!(\\\"INSERT INTO play_campaign_content_tags(campaign_id,content_id,tag,position) VALUES ({},{},{},{});\\\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(content_id), sql_text(tag), position))?;\\n Ok(PlayContentList::Found { content, is_owner })\\n    }\\n\\n    fn play_calendar_owner(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str) -> Result<Option<bool>, ()> {\\n        let owner = Mutable in-memory state is saved immediately\\n/// after every successful auth or combat mutation; relational domains commit in\\n/// their storage methods.\\nfn handle(\\n    stream: &mut TcpStream,\\n    sessions: &mut Sessions,\\n    users: &mut Users,\\n    storage: &mut Storage,\\n) -> std::io::Result<()> {\\n    let request = read_request(stream)?;\\n    let Some(HttpRequest {\\n        method,\\n        path,\\n        body,\\n        authorization,\\n    }) = request\\n    else {\\n        return respond(stream, 400, r#\\\"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"bad request\\\"}\\\"#);\\n    };\\n    if (method == \\\"POST\\\" || method == \\\"PUT\\\" || method == \\\"GET\\\") && path.starts_with(\\\"/v1/play/campaigns/\\\") {\\n        let Some((actor_name, _)) = authenticated_user(authorization.as_deref(), users) else {\\n            return respond(stream, 401, r#\\\"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"unauthorized\\\"}\\\"#);\\n        };\\n        if method == \\\"POST\\\" {\\n            if let Some(campaign_id) = path.strip_prefix(\\\"/v1/play/campaigns/\\\").and_then(|value| value.strip_suffix(\\\"/content\\\")).filter(|id| !id.contains('/')) {\\n                return play_content_response(create_play_content(campaign_id, actor_name, &body, storage), stream, 201);\\n            }\\n        }\\n        if method == \\\"PUT\\\" {\\n            if let Some((campaign_id, content_id)) = path.strip_prefix(\\\"/v1/play/campaigns/\\\").and_then(|value| value.split_once(\\\"/content/\\\")).and_then(|(campaign_id, value)| value.strip_suffix(\\\"/tags\\\").map(|content_id| (campaign_id, content_id))).filter(|(campaign_id, content_id)| !campaign_id.contains('/') && !content_id.contains('/')) {\\n                return play_content_response(update_play_content_tags(campaign_id, actor_name, content_id, &body, storage), stream, 200);\\n            }\\n        }\\n        if method == \\\"GET\\\" {\\n            let (resource, query) = path.split_once('?').unwrap_or((&path, \\\"\\\"));\\n            if let Some(campaign_id) = resource.strip_prefix(\\\"/v1/play/campaigns/\\\").and_then(|value| value.strip_suffix(\\\"/content\\\")).filter(|id| !id.contains('/')) {\\n                let exclude_tag = if query.is_empty() { Ok(None) } else if let Some(tag) = query.strip_prefix(\\\"exclude_tag=\\\").filter(|tag| !tag.is_empty() && !tag.contains('&')) { Ok(Some(tag)) } else { Err(PlayContentError::Invalid) };\\n                return play_content_response(exclude_tag.and_then(|tag| get_play_content(campaign_id, actor_name, tag, storage)), stream, 200);\\n            }\\n        }\\n    }\\n    if (method == \\\"PUT\\\" || method == \\\"GET\\\") && path.starts_with(\\\"/v1/play/campaigns/\\\") {\\n        if let Some(campaign_id) = path.strip_prefix(\\\"/v1/play/campaigns/\\\").and_then(|p| p.strip_suffix(\\\"/session-zero\\\")) {\\n            let Some((actor_name, _)) = authenticated_user(authorization.as_deref(), users) else {\\n                return respond(stream, 401, r#\\\"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"unauthorized\\\"}\\\"#);\\n            };\\n            if method == \\\"PUT\\\" {\\n                return match update_play_session_zero(campaign_id, actor_name, &body, storage) {\\n                    Ok(response) => respond(stream, 200, &response),\\n                    Err(PlaySessionZeroError::Invalid) => respond(stream, 400, r#\\\"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"invalid request\\\"}\\\"#),\\n                    Err(PlaySessionZeroError::UnknownCampaign | PlaySessionZeroError::Missing) => respond(stream, 404, r#\\\"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"not found\\\"}\\\"#),\\n                    Err(PlaySessionZeroError::Forbidden) => respond(stream, 403, r#\\\"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"forbidden\\\"}\\\"#),\\n                    Err(PlaySessionZeroError::Conflict) => respond(stream, 409, r#\\\"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"conflict\\\"}\\\"#),\\n                    Err(PlaySessionZeroError::Storage) => respond(stream, 500, r#\\\"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"storage failure\\\"}\\\"#),\\n                };\\n            }\\n            return match get_play_session_zero(campaign_id, actor_name, storage) {\\n                Ok(response) => respond(stream, 200, &response),\\n                Err(PlayActionError::Invalid) => {\\n                    respond(stream, 400, r#\\\"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"invalid request\\\"}\\\"#)\\n                }\\n                Err(PlayActionError::UnknownCampaign) => {\\n                    respond(stream, 404, r#\\\"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"not found\\\"}\\\"#)\\n                }\\n                Err(PlayActionError::Forbidden) => respond(stream, 403, r#\\\"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"forbidden\\\"}\\\"#),\\n                Err(PlayActionError::Conflict) => respond(stream, 409, r#\\\"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"conflict\\\"}\\\"#),\\n                Err(PlayActionError::Storage) => {\\n                    respond(stream, 500, r#\\\"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"storage failure\\\"}\\\"#)\\n                }\\n            };\\n        }\\n        if let Some(id) = path\\n            .strip_prefix(\\\"/v1/play/campaigns/\\\")\\n            .and_then(|p| p.strip_suffix(\\\"/narrations\\\"))\\n        {\\n            let Some((actor_name, actor_role)) =\\n                authenticated_user(authorization.as_deref(), users)\\n            else {\\n                return respond(stream, 401, r#\\\"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"unauthorized\\\"}\\\"#);\\n            };\\n            if actor_role != \\\"dm\\\" {\\n                return respond(stream, 403, campaign\\\",\\n# D&D REST service\\n\\n## Run and verify\\n\\nThe server targets Rust 1.97.0 and uses the Rust standard library only. Its\\ndurable store is accessed through the locally installed `sqlite3` executable.\\nStart it in the foreground with a port selected through `PORT`:\\n\\n```sh\\nPORT=8080 ./run.sh\\n```\\n\\nIt listens only on `127.0.0.1`. In another terminal, check readiness with:\\n\\n```sh\\ncurl -i http://127.0.0.1:8080/health\\n```\\n\\nThe successful response body is `{\\\"ok\\\":true}`. Run the regression suite without\\nstarting the server using `cargo test`. `run.sh` deliberately blocks after it\\nexecutes the compiled server, so it is not a build-only check.\\n\\n## Files and request flow\\n\\n- `run.sh` compiles `src/main.rs` with Rust 2024 edition and runs the server in\\n  the foreground.\\n- `Cargo.toml` declares the dependency-free crate and test target.\\n- `src/main.rs` contains the complete application, organized into data types,\\n  the `Storage` persistence adapter, state encoding, HTTP startup/routing,\\n  domain handlers, JSON parsing, response writing, and focused unit tests.\\n\\n`main` binds the listener, opens `game.db`, restores the in-memory users and\\ncombat sessions, then processes one TCP connection at a time. `read_request`\\nis the HTTP framing boundary: it accepts a bounded header/body and returns an\\nowned `HttpRequest`. `handle` is the routing boundary. It checks parameterized\\nresource routes before exact method/path routes, while the focused helper\\nfunctions below it implement the individual domain operations. `respond` writes\\nevery JSON HTTP response.\\n\\nThe custom `Json` parser and its `*_field` helpers form the validation boundary.\\nResponse JSON is assembled in fixed field order; preserve that ordering because\\nit is observable and keeps output deterministic without `serde`.\\n\\n## State and persistence\\n\\n`Storage` is the only persistence adapter. It creates the schema and invokes\\nthe platform `sqlite3` executable for relational reads and writes, so the Rust\\ncrate itself remains dependency-free. `game.db` contains:\\n\\n- compendium tables for monsters, tags, and items;\\n- campaign tables for characters, events, quests/milestones, factions, NPCs,\\n  inventory/equipment, crafting projects, and scheduled sessions/attendance;\\n- play-campaign tables for memberships, runtime turns, documents, events, and\\n  turn nudges;\\n- `game_state`, a versioned BLOB for registered users and combat sessions; and\\n- `schema_meta`, currently schema version 1.\\n\\nThe `DNDSTATE1`/`DNDSTATE2` encoder sorts map keys before saving. Preserve its\\nformat and validation: it is read on the next server start. It contains only\\nregistered users and standalone combat sessions; successful auth and combat\\nmutations save it immediately. Relational-domain methods write directly through\\n`Storage`. `/v1/storage/reset` recreates the tables and clears the corresponding\\nin-memory state.\\n\\n## API/domain groupings\\n\\n- Core rules: health, storage status/reset, dice, ability checks, encounter XP,\\n  and initiative.\\n- Characters and PHB rules: ability modifiers, proficiency, derived stats,\\n  spell slots, long rests, and equipment load.\\n- Authentication and combat: registration/login plus combat sessions, turns,\\n  and conditions.\\n- Compendium: monster and item creation with slug lookups.\\n- Campaign management: campaigns, characters, events, quests, factions, NPCs,\\n  inventory, equipment, crafting, schedules, analytics, audits, exports, and\\n  relationship/state summaries.\\n- Play campaigns: DM-owned lobbies, player membership, campaign documents,\\n  narrated turns, player actions, GM resolutions, nudges, and player/GM turn\\n  context.\\n- DM tools: encounter building, deterministic tier-1 loot parcels, and session\\n  recaps derived from persisted campaign and compendium data.\\n\\n## Safe extension and testing conventions\\n\\nKeep the service stdlib-only: use `TcpListener`/`TcpStream`, the existing JSON\\nhelpers, and no Cargo dependencies or HTTP crates. Treat exact paths, response\\nfield order, error bodies/statuses, validation limits, and persistence formats\\nas public behavior.\\n\\nAdd a route in `handle`, keep its domain logic in a focused helper, and add the\\ncorresponding persistence operation to `Storage` instead of embedding SQL in a\\nhandler. Unit tests live in the `#[cfg(test)]` module at the end of\\n`src/main.rs`; add focused assertions there, run `cargo test`, and exercise\\npersistence-sensitive routes with `curl` against a fresh database. Do not start\\n`./run.sh` for a build-only campaign_id: &str,\\n        actor: &str,\\n    ) -> Result<PlaySessionZeroRead, ()> {\\n        let owner = self.sql(&format!(\\n            \\\"SELECT hex(owner) FROM play_campaigns WHERE id = {};\\\",\\n            sql_text(campaign_id)\\n        ))?;\\n        let owner = owner.trim();\\n        if owner.is_empty() {\\n            return Ok(PlaySessionZeroRead::UnknownCampaign);\\n        }\\n        if decode_sql_text(owner)? != actor && self.sql(&format!(\\n            \\\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_members WHERE campaign_id = {} AND username = {};\\\",\\n            sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(actor)\\n        ))?.trim().is_empty() {\\n            return Ok(PlaySessionZeroRead::Forbidden);\\n        }\\n        let row = self.sql(&format!(\\n            \\\"SELECT hex(rules),hex(tone) FROM play_campaign_session_zero WHERE campaign_id = {};\\\",\\n            sql_text(campaign_id)\\n        ))?;\\n        let mut fields = row.trim().split('|');\\n        let Some(rules_hex) = fields.next().filter(|value| !value.is_empty()) else {\\n            return Ok(PlaySessionZeroRead::Missing);\\n        };\\n        let rules = decode_sql_text(rules_hex)?;\\n        let tone = decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?;\\n        if fields.next().is_some() {\\n            return Err(());\\n        }\\n        let consent = ))?.lines().map(decode_sql_text).collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;\\n        Ok(PlaySessionZeroRead::Found(PlaySessionZeroSettings { rules, tone, consent }))\\n    }\\n\\n    fn create_play_content(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str, content: &PlayContent) -> Result<PlayContentCreate, ()> {\\n        let owner = self.sql(&format!(\\\"SELECT hex(owner) FROM play_campaigns WHERE id = {};\\\", sql_text(campaign_id)))?;\\n        let owner = owner.trim();\\n        if owner.is_empty() { return Ok(PlayContentCreate::UnknownCampaign); }\\n        if decode_sql_text(owner)? != actor { return Ok(PlayContentCreate::Forbidden); }\\n        if !self.sql(&format!(\\\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_content WHERE campaign_id = {} AND content_id = {};\\\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(&content.content_id)))?.trim().is_empty() {\\n            return Ok(PlayContentCreate::Duplicate);\\n        }\\n        let position = self.sql(&format!(\\\"SELECT COALESCE(MAX(position) + 1, 0) FROM play_campaign_content WHERE campaign_id = {};\\\", sql_text(campaign_id)))?.trim().parse::<i64>().map_err(|_| ())?;\\n        self.sql(&format!(\\\"INSERT INTO play_campaign_content(campaign_id,content_id,kind,text,position) VALUES ({},{},{},{},{});\\\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(&content.content_id), sql_text(&content.kind), sql_text(&content.text), position))?;\\n        for (position, tag) in content.tags.iter().enumerate() {\\n            self.sql(&format!(\\\"INSERT INTO play_campaign_content_tags(campaign_id,content_id,tag,position) VALUES ({},{},{},{});\\\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(&content.content_id), sql_text(tag), position))?;\\n        }\\n        Ok(PlayContentCreate::Created)\\n    }\\n\\n    fn update_play_content_tags(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str, content_id: &str, tags: &[String]) -> Result<PlayContentUpdate, ()> {\\n        let owner = self.sql(&format!(\\\"SELECT hex(owner) FROM play_campaigns WHERE id = {};\\\", sql_text(campaign_id)))?;\\n        let owner = owner.trim();\\n        if owner.is_empty() { return Ok(PlayContentUpdate::UnknownCampaign); }\\n        if decode_sql_text(owner)? != actor { return Ok(PlayContentUpdate::Forbidden); }\\n        if self.sql(&format!(\\\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_content WHERE campaign_id = {} AND content_id = {};\\\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(content_id)))?.trim().is_empty() {\\n            return Ok(PlayContentUpdate::Missing);\\n        }\\n        self.sql(&format!(\\\"DELETE FROM play_campaign_content_tags WHERE campaign_id = {} AND content_id = {};\\\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(content_id)))?;\\n        for (position, tag) in tags.iter().enumerate() {\\n            self.sql(&format!(\\\"INSERT INTO play_campaign_content_tags(campaign_id,content_id,tag,position) VALUES ({},{},{},{});\\\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(content_id), sql_text(tag), position))?;\\n        }\\n        Ok(PlayContentUpdate::Updated)\\n    }\\n\\n    fn play_content(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str) -> Result<PlayContentList, ()> {\\n        let owner = self.sql(&format!(\\\"SELECT hex(owner) FROM play_campaigns WHERE id = {};\\\", sql_text(campaign_id)))?;\\n        let owner = owner.trim();\\n        if owner.is_empty() { return Ok(PlayContentList::UnknownCampaign); }\\n        let is_owner = decode_sql_text(owner)? == actor;\\n        if !is_owner && self.sql(&format!(\\\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_members WHERE campaign_id = {} AND username = {};\\\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(actor)))?.trim().is_empty() {\\n            return Ok(PlayContentList::Forbidden);\\n        }\\n        let rows = self.sql(&format!(\\\"SELECT hex(content_id),hex(kind),hex(text) FROM play_campaign_content WHERE campaign_id = {} ORDER BY position;\\\", sql_text(campaign_id)))?;\\n        let mut content = Vec::new();\\n        for row in rows.lines() {\\n            let mut fields = row.split('|');\\n            let content_id = decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?;\\n            let kind = decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?;\\n            let text = decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?;\\n            if fields.next().is_some() { return Err(()); }\\n            let tags = self.sql(&format!(\\\"SELECT hex(tag) FROM play_campaign_content_tags WHERE campaign_id = {} AND content_id = {} ORDER BY position;\\\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(&content_id)))?.lines().map(decode_sql_text).collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;\\n            content.push(PlayContent { content_id, kind, text, tags });\\n        }\\n        Ok(PlayContentList::Found { content, is_owner })\\n    }\\n\\n    fn play_calendar_owner(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str) -> Result<Option<bool>, ()> {\\n        let owner = Ok(PlayCalendarResult::UnknownCampaign),\\n            Some(false) => return Ok(PlayCalendarResult::Forbidden),\\n            Some(true) => {}\\n    Ok(())\\n}\\n\\n/// Route one fully read request.  Mutable in-memory state is saved immediately\\n/// after every successful auth or combat mutation; relational domains commit in\\n/// their storage methods.\\nfn handle(\\n    stream: &mut TcpStream,\\n    sessions: &mut Sessions,\\n    users: &mut Users,\\n    storage: &mut Storage,\\n) -> std::io::Result<()> {\\n    let request = read_request(stream)?;\\n    let Some(HttpRequest {\\n        method,\\n        path,\\n        body,\\n        authorization,\\n    }) = request\\n    else {\\n        return respond(stream, 400, r#\\\"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"bad request\\\"}\\\"#);\\n    };\\n    if (method == \\\"POST\\\" || method == \\\"PUT\\\" || method == \\\"GET\\\") && path.starts_with(\\\"/v1/play/campaigns/\\\") {\\n        let Some((actor_name, _)) = authenticated_user(authorization.as_deref(), users) else {\\n            return respond(stream, 401, r#\\\"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"unauthorized\\\"}\\\"#);\\n        };\\n        if method == \\\"POST\\\" {\\n            if let Some(campaign_id) = path.strip_prefix(\\\"/v1/play/campaigns/\\\").and_then(|value| value.strip_suffix(\\\"/content\\\")).filter(|id| !id.contains('/')) {\\n                return play_content_response(create_play_content(campaign_id, actor_name, &body, storage), stream, 201);\\n            }\\n        }\\n        if method == \\\"PUT\\\" {\\n            if let Some((campaign_id, content_id)) = path.strip_prefix(\\\"/v1/play/campaigns/\\\").and_then(|value| value.split_once(\\\"/content/\\\")).and_then(|(campaign_id, value)| value.strip_suffix(\\\"/tags\\\").map(|content_id| (campaign_id, content_id))).filter(|(campaign_id, content_id)| !campaign_id.contains('/') && !content_id.contains('/')) {\\n                return play_content_response(update_play_content_tags(campaign_id, actor_name, content_id, &body, storage), stream, 200);\\n            }\\n        }\\n        if method == \\\"GET\\\" {\\n            let
